- **session-closeout**: Close work sessions with durable receipts. - **session-closeout**: Close out a working session into a durable, structured handoff so engineering judgment compounds across sessions instead of evaporating. Use when the user signals the end of a work block — "wrap up", "close out", "session summary", "handoff", "what did we do", "pick this up later", "end of day", "hand this off" — and you need to capture decisions made, work completed, open threads, and concrete next steps before context is lost. Not a general mid-session summarizer; it produces a single reproducible handoff document, not memory writes or commits. ([Syed007Hassan/MethodForge](https://github.com/Syed007Hassan/MethodForge/tree/HEAD/skills/session-closeout))

- **session-closer**: 会话收尾 —— 在任意对话(含未走完十步循环的探索/调试/讨论 session)把"未交接成果" 固化为 handoff。**与十步循环正交平级的会话仪式**(非周期收尾): AI 先内省本对话出 未完成线程 + 待固化经验, 再用机械 autofill 交叉核验补漏, 写 docs/handoff/。leaf — 终结于写交接, 不拖入十步循环。 使用场景: "对话收尾" / "执行对话收尾" / "会话收尾" / "session closeout" / "收尾这次对话" / "写交接" / "写 handoff" / "收工" / "结束本次对话" / context 快满时主动收尾。 不适用 (用 phase-d-closer): "Phase D" / "周期收尾" / "归档 Spec" / "更新 cycle 进度" —— 那是开发周期收尾, 不是会话收尾。 ([10CG/aria-plugin](https://github.com/10CG/aria-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/session-closer) · ★1)

Proactively suggests handoffs after substantial work. Stores handoffs in memory/handoffs, never .claude/handoffs. ([simtransforma/HAOS-Codex-Model](https://github.com/simtransforma/HAOS-Codex-Model/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/session-handoff))
- **session-handoff**: セッションを次のセッションへ引き継ぐ手順。引き継ぎ用の PR を作成し、最初の仕事の有無と正確な残件一覧を渡すことで完成する（`/handoff`）。 ([sm06224/PKC3](https://github.com/sm06224/PKC3/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/session-handoff))
- **session-handoff**: Package the current session into a self-contained handoff that a fresh session, possibly on another machine, can act on cold. Use when the user says "hand this off", "write a handoff", or wants to continue this work in a new session or on another machine. Not for recapping the current session (session-recap). ([smorinlabs/smorinlabs-harness](https://github.com/smorinlabs/smorinlabs-harness/tree/HEAD/plugins/session/skills/session-handoff))
- **session-handoff**: Package the current session into a self-contained handoff that a fresh session, possibly on another machine, can act on cold. Use when the user says "hand this off", "write a handoff", or wants to continue this work in a new session or on another machine. Not for recapping the current session (session-recap), launching a side idea in a new Rex tab (session-spinoff), or moving this same conversation to another Rex session (session-move). ([smorinlabs/smorinlabs-skills](https://github.com/smorinlabs/smorinlabs-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/session/skills/session-handoff))
- **session-handoff**: Writes the resume note a fresh session needs to take over — what is running where with ETA and log path, what is confirmed with numbers, the exact next steps in order, the fallback state that is certain, today's traps, and the scratchpad tools copied into the repo before they vanish. Use it whenever a session may end with work in flight — a long background job is running when the turn ends, the context is getting long, the user says they are stepping away, or a deadline week has jobs on remote nodes. Triggers include "이어받을 수 있게", "인계 문서", "세션 끊기기 전에", "resume 문서", "지금 상태 정리해둬", "다음 세션이 볼 수 있게… ([SMU-ReinforementLearning/FlyTracker](https://github.com/SMU-ReinforementLearning/FlyTracker/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/session-handoff) · 2 copies)
- **session-handoff**: Use when reading, creating, continuing, or appending to the current session handoff under .session-continuity/handoffs/. ([snjnlsn/snjnlsn-marketplace](https://github.com/snjnlsn/snjnlsn-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/session-continuity/skills/session-handoff))
- **session-handoff**: Use when Bryan explicitly asks for a handoff he won't supervise from here: "status-only", "just tell me when it's done", "fire and forget", or work whose acceptance a later session owns. Reconciles the vault record, decides whether the frontier is one separable slice or several, and starts a background agent per slice. A plain "hand off" or "hand this off" is not this skill: that is a supervised background agent (coding-agent-handoff-supervision). Not for "launch a next session" (next-session). ([SnowboardTechie/dotfiles](https://github.com/SnowboardTechie/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/dot-agents/skills/session-handoff))
- **session-handoff**: Use when ending research/planning, compacting noisy context, or handing off work to another session/lane. ([spw-m-riley/copilot-stuff](https://github.com/spw-m-riley/copilot-stuff/tree/HEAD/skills/session-handoff))
- **session-handoff**: Write or resume a mid-phase handoff for sessiondeck when a session's context is getting large or the user wants to stop. - **session-handoff**: AI 세션을 종료·중단하거나 컨텍스트 한계가 가까울 때, 다음 세션(또는 다른 팀원의 AI)이 이어받을 인수인계 노트를 남길 때 사용한다. 새 세션이 진행 중 작업을 이어받을 때도 이 노트부터 읽는다. ([Sungw0o/Harness](https://github.com/Sungw0o/Harness/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/session-handoff) · 2 copies)
- **session-handoff**: Use this skill when the user explicitly wants to summarize, wrap up, or hand off the current AI coding session into a concise Markdown document for a future session. Trigger phrases include: "session handoff", "handoff doc", "generate a handoff document", "summarize this session for the next session", "distill this session into a handoff md". - **session-handoff**: Gera um handoff extremamente detalhado da sessão atual, salvo em `.session-artifacts/HANDOFF.md` (arquivo local, nunca commitado), pra minimizar perda de contexto ao migrar pra uma sessão nova do Claude. Ao contrário de um resumo denso, esta skill prioriza completude sobre brevidade: reconstrói a timeline cronológica da sessão, lista todo arquivo tocado, registra tentativas que falharam (pra não repetir), convenções e preferências estabelecidas, e anexa um snapshot real do git (status/diff/log) como fonte da verdade. Use quando o usuário pedir "gera um handoff", "salva o contexto pra próxima… ([Takkuya/claude-skills](https://github.com/Takkuya/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/session-handoff))
- **session-handoff**: ハーネス導入済みプロジェクトでセッションを終える・/clear する・長い区切りを付ける前に、docs/handoff.md と関連 docs を「次のセッションが session-catchup しても実態とズレない状態」に更新し、未コミットの正史 doc を救出し、harness status を報告する。発火例 - "引き継ぎを書いて", "handoff を更新して", "セッションを畳む準備", "wrap up", "write a handoff", "/clear する前に状態を残して"。 ([takumifukasawa/agent-harness](https://github.com/takumifukasawa/agent-harness/tree/HEAD/harness/skills/session-handoff))
